DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!
WEST_FIRST
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!

DUATO_BASELINE
copy all verilog files in rtl_work folder
/home/alireza/Mywork/mpsoc/script/verilator_multiple/duato vc_alloc_request_gen.v have been replaced
split all verilog modules in separate  files
#Create object files
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include /usr/share/verilator/include/verilated.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vrouter.cpp Vrouter__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vnoc.cpp Vnoc__Syms.cpp -O3
g++ -c -I. -I/usr/share/verilator/include Vtraffic.cpp Vtraffic__Syms.cpp -O3
#Link object files into static library
ar rcs libVrouter.a Vrouter.o Vrouter__Syms.o verilated.o 
ar rcs libVnoc.a Vnoc.o Vnoc__Syms.o 
ar rcs libVtraffic.a Vtraffic.o Vtraffic__Syms.o 
#Remove object files
rm *.o
#Compile your test code
g++ -I. -L. -I/usr/share/verilator/include -o testbench testbench.cpp -lVrouter -lVnoc -lVtraffic  -Wall -O3
done!
